# CactusFS — PLAN

Source of truth for **what** is being built and **where we are**. Rules live in
`CLAUDE.md`; the design lives in `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`.

Status values: `todo | in_progress | done | blocked`.
A step is `done` only when its acceptance criterion holds **and** `make check` is green.

## Milestone 0 — Scaffold

Done when `make verify-clone` and `make check` are green.

| ID | Status | Step | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0.1 | done | Plan, architecture, threat model, agent definitions | `PLAN.md` covers milestones 0–8 with IDs, criteria and files; docs written fresh from `CLAUDE.md` | `PLAN.md`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/threat-model.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`, `.claude/agents/{planner,coder,reviewer}.md` |
| 0.2 | done | Go module and hexagonal skeleton with the health use case | `go build ./...`, `go vet ./...`, `go test ./...` pass with Go alone; `internal/domain` imports nothing outside the standard library; domain and app coverage ≥ 80 % | `go.mod`, `go.sum`, `internal/domain/errors.go`, `internal/domain/cluster/health.go`, `internal/ports/health.go`, `internal/app/health.go`, tests |
| 0.3 | done | `cactusfs` binary: `version` and `server` | `cactusfs version` prints version, Go version and OS/arch; `cactusfs server` serves only `GET /healthz` and `GET /readyz`, over TLS 1.2+ or (with `--dev-plaintext`) on loopback only; config from `CACTUSFS_*` env with matching flags; JSON logs on stdout; on SIGTERM `/readyz` answers 503 for `--drain-delay`, then the listener closes and in-flight requests finish within `--shutdown-timeout` | `cmd/cactusfs/{main,config,server,version}.go`, `internal/adapters/health/{handler,gate}.go`, tests, `test/integration/server_test.go` |
| 0.4 | done | Multi-stage `Dockerfile` | `docker build .` yields a distroless, non-root image < 60 MB for linux/amd64 and linux/arm64; every base image pinned by digest; `--target builder` yields the tool image | `Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore` |
| 0.5 | done | `Makefile` and gate scripts | every target in `CLAUDE.md` §6 exists; targets run inside the builder image with `ENGINE=docker\|podman`; plain `go build/test/vet` work without `make` | `Makefile`, `scripts/*.sh` (one per gate, plus `no-bypass.sh`), `scripts/web-licences.mjs`, `.golangci.yml`, `.gitignore`, `.gitattributes`, `test/s3compat/{doc.go,baseline.txt}` |
| 0.6 | done | Console scaffold | `pnpm -C web lint && typecheck && test && build` clean; no CDN or other remote asset; favicon linked and logo taken from the brand assets, unmodified | `web/**` (brand files in `docs/brand/`, `web/public/`, `web/src/assets/brand/` were supplied by Albie) |
| 0.7 | done | CI matrix | workflow runs `make check` on Linux and unit tests on Linux, macOS and Windows; actions pinned by commit SHA | `.github/workflows/ci.yml` |
| 0.8 | done | README | build-from-source steps for Go-only and container-only builds, run instructions | `README.md` |
| 0.9 | done | Verification and review | `make verify-clone` and `make check` green (darwin/arm64 host, Docker 29.8.1, linux/arm64 builder); reviewer: 3 blocker, 3 major fixed; 12 minor fixed or raised as open questions | — |

### Gates and what each fails without

In milestone 0 four gates guarded artefacts that did not exist yet, and passed while
there was nothing to check. Every subject exists now, and none of them may pass without
it (verified against the scripts on 2026-10-12; the Decisions row of that date says what
was still left over and was fixed):

| Gate | Fails without |
|---|---|
| `make contracts` | generated code in sync; `api/openapi/admin.yaml`; at least one document in `api/asyncapi`; `api/proto` that lints and is formatted; a reference commit (`CONTRACT_BASE`, or `HEAD`) that exists **and holds** the management API and the node RPC to compare with - a reference without them fails, it is no longer "nothing to compare with" |
| `make s3-compat` | `test/s3compat/baseline.txt` with at least one test recorded; a suite that builds and runs; every recorded test passing; `TestCephS3Tests` and `TestAWSCLICopyUpAndDown` in the baseline where the tools are built in |
| `make helm-lint` | `deploy/helm/cactusfs/Chart.yaml` (no "no chart yet"); an `appVersion` that is `internal/buildinfo/VERSION`; at least one value set in `ci/` and one in `ci/refused/`; every render valid, asserted, and every refusal refused for the reason it names |
| `make licences` | at least one third-party module linked on each of the six platforms; at least one production package of the console |
| `make observability-check` | the rules, their tests and the chart's copy; at least one dashboard; at least one value set in `ci/monitoring/`; the four Go tests it runs, each found by name before it is run (`go test -run` is content with a name that matches nothing) |
| `make cover`, `make fmt-check`, `make lint` | measured statements in `internal/domain` and `internal/app`; Go files to read |
| `make test-int` | black-box tests of the real binary, of a four-node cluster of product-image containers and of Vault (testcontainers-go). NATS joins with 8.2 - the one subject that does not exist yet; nothing stands in for it |

`make up`, `make down`, `make test-chaos`, `make test-rolling` and `make test-k8s` are
implemented (3.8; 2.8 and 3.9; G.6; 7.10) and are not part of `make check`; the last three
are CI jobs of their own. `make test-rolling` fails when the test it names is not there.

Still tolerated, on purpose: `scripts/release.sh --dry-run` writes no `SHA256SUMS` when
`dist/` holds no binary and says so (inside `make check` the binaries are always built
first; `--publish` requires all six).

## Milestone 1 — Standalone store

Done when a single `cactusfs server --data <dir>` passes the milestone's S3 compatibility
subset with encryption on.

| ID | Status | Step | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1.1 | done | Domain model: bucket, object, version, IDs, typed errors | naming rules per the S3 reference; property tests; no external imports | `internal/domain/{bucket,object}/`, `internal/domain/errors.go` |
| 1.2 | done | Ports: `ObjectIndex`, `BucketStore`, `RootCredentialStore`, `ShardStore`, `KMS`, `Clock`, `Authorizer` | interfaces documented; fakes and contract tests in `internal/ports/porttest`, run against fakes and real adapters alike | `internal/ports/`, `internal/domain/iam/` |
| 1.3 | done | Disk adapter | shards stored under generated IDs only, all I/O through `os.Root`; exclusive drive lock; write + file fsync + directory fsync per OS; versioned header with checksum; position-bound SHA-256 per block verified on every read; ADR for the format | `internal/adapters/disk/`, `docs/adr/0001-on-disk-format.md` |
| 1.4 | done | Streaming AEAD and envelope encryption | AES-256-GCM segments, unique nonce, segment index in AAD; truncation/reorder detected; fuzz + property tests; ADR | `internal/domain/object/crypto*`, `internal/domain/iam/secret.go`, `docs/adr/0002-encryption.md` |
| 1.5 | done | Sealed local key file KMS (explicit opt-in) | no unencrypted fallback; key file sealed with a passphrase-derived key (Argon2id) | `internal/adapters/kms/local/` |
| 1.6 | done | Single-node Raft metadata (bbolt) | bucket and object index committed through Raft; versioned, typed entries; each entry applied exactly once across restarts and snapshots; ADR | `internal/adapters/raftmeta/`, `docs/adr/0003-metadata.md` |
| 1.7 | done | Use cases: bucket create/delete/list, object PUT/GET/HEAD/DELETE, ListObjectsV2 | write path order per §2; table-driven tests; coverage ≥ 80 % | `internal/app/` |
| 1.8 | done | S3 API adapter with SigV4 (header auth) and XML errors | path-style and virtual-hosted-style; literal, `UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD` and `STREAMING-UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD-TRAILER` (aws-chunked) payloads with checksum verification; fuzz tests for SigV4, XML and chunk decoding; constant-time comparison | `internal/adapters/s3api/` |
| 1.9 | done | Root bootstrap, `--data`, `--meta-dir`, KMS flags and wiring | first start requires or generates root credentials, shown once; no default credentials | `cmd/cactusfs/{config,node,server}.go`, `internal/app/identity.go` |
| 1.10 | done | S3 compatibility suite, first baseline | aws-sdk-go-v2 with default options over TLS against the built binary; one top-level test per behaviour, recorded in `baseline.txt` | `test/s3compat/` |
| 1.11 | done | Telemetry adapter | authenticated `/metrics` (`cactusfs_*`, own registry, no tenant labels) and OpenTelemetry traces as decorators on every port; no exporter unless an OTLP endpoint is configured | `internal/adapters/telemetry/` |

## Milestone 2 — Erasure coding and integrity on local drives

Done when a standalone node with four or more drives survives the loss or corruption of
up to *parity* drives without a failed read, repairs itself, and `make test-chaos` is
green.

| ID | Status | Step | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2.1 | done | Erasure domain: parameters, quorum, stripe geometry, set layout | parity 2/3/4 by set size, 1+0 below four drives; write quorum = data (+1 when data = parity); sets of 4–16 drives, equal size; property tests on the geometry | `internal/domain/erasure/` |
| 2.2 | done | Reed-Solomon codec behind `ports.ErasureCodec` | `klauspost/reedsolomon`; property test: encode → lose ≤ parity shards → decode equals original; fuzz; contract test | `internal/ports/erasure.go`, `internal/adapters/erasurecodec/` |
| 2.3 | done | Topology in metadata and striped storage | drive slots recorded through Raft and addressed by drive ID, so reordering `--data` is harmless; shards of one object never share a drive; write succeeds on a write quorum and fails below it; reads reconstruct from any *data* shards; ADR | `internal/adapters/raftmeta/`, `internal/app/storage.go`, `internal/app/topology.go`, `docs/adr/0004-erasure-and-topology.md` |
| 2.4 | done | Heal | a degraded read queues the version; healing rebuilds missing, truncated and corrupt shards from the others and verifies the object is still current; a replaced (empty) drive is adopted into its slot and refilled | `internal/app/heal.go` |
| 2.5 | done | Background scrubber | walks every object, verifies every shard block, heals what it finds; rate-limited; `--scrub-interval`; metrics | `internal/app/scrub.go`, `cmd/cactusfs/` |
| 2.6 | done | Fewer than four drives | 1+0 on one drive each, encrypted and checksummed; logs that there is no parity | `internal/domain/erasure/`, `cmd/cactusfs/` |
| 2.7 | done | Garbage collection of unreferenced shards | `ShardStore.List`; a data-ID index in the state machine; shards older than `--gc-grace` that no version refers to are removed | `internal/ports/shards.go`, `internal/adapters/disk/`, `internal/adapters/raftmeta/`, `internal/app/gc.go` |
| 2.8 | done | Chaos suite | against the built binary with six drives: corrupt shards, delete shards, wipe up to *parity* drives → every read is correct and the drives are repaired; one more than parity → reads fail, never wrong data; `make test-chaos` | `test/chaos/`, `Makefile` |

## Milestone 3 — Cluster

Design (ADR-0005): every node runs Raft — `meta` nodes as voters, the others as
non-voters — so every node holds the metadata locally. Changes are forwarded to the
leader over node RPC; reads wait for the leader's read index. Shards are reached through
`ports.ShardStore` whether the drive is local or on another node. A cluster is formed
once, from the complete list of its nodes (`--peers`); its erasure sets are planned
across nodes, racks and zones at that moment.

| ID | Status | Step | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 3.1 | done | Cluster domain: nodes, roles, topology labels | `--roles` selects gateway/meta/storage/console; `region/zone/rack/host` labels; topology planned across nodes, then racks/zones; a node answers for its own drives only | `internal/domain/cluster/`, `cmd/cactusfs/` |
| 3.2 | done | Node RPC over mTLS 1.3 | gRPC contract in `api/proto/`; generated code committed; `buf lint` and `buf breaking` in `make contracts`; a remote drive passes the same `ShardStore` contract as a local one; every call passes the authorizer as a node principal | `api/proto/`, `internal/adapters/noderpc/`, `internal/adapters/nodetls/`, `buf.yaml`, `buf.gen.yaml`, `scripts/{gen,contracts}.sh`, `Dockerfile` |
| 3.3 | done | Multi-node Raft (3 or 5 voters) over mTLS | formation from identical peer lists, join, leave, leader change, linearizable reads on every node; a node never logs an entry newer than the oldest node understands (rolling upgrade N ↔ N+1 test) | `internal/adapters/raftmeta/`, `docs/adr/0005-cluster.md` |
| 3.4 | done | Distributed erasure sets | shards of one object on different nodes where the cluster allows; the tolerated number of node failures is computed and logged | `internal/domain/cluster/topology.go`, `internal/app/topology.go`, `internal/ports/drives.go` |
| 3.5 | done | Quorum write and read paths | no acknowledgement before write quorum + Raft commit; reads and writes survive nodes that are down up to parity; an unreachable drive is neither damage nor a reason to delete; read-after-write on every node | `internal/app/`, `internal/domain/errors.go` |
| 3.6 | done | Events from applied Raft entries | contract first in `api/asyncapi/events.yaml`, AsyncAPI validation in `make contracts`; in-process bus; consumers dedupe by event ID | `api/asyncapi/events.yaml`, `scripts/contracts.sh`, `internal/ports/events.go`, `internal/adapters/events/inproc/` |
| 3.7 | done | Integration tests on a real cluster | testcontainers-go: four containers of the product image form a cluster over mTLS; S3 through every node | `test/integration/` |
| 3.8 | done | Compose deployment | 4-node cluster; unchanged on `docker compose` and rootless `podman compose`; `make up/down`; `cactusfs dev-init` writes the throw-away CA, certificates and shared key file | `deploy/compose/compose.yaml`, `cmd/cactusfs/devinit.go`, `Makefile` |
| 3.9 | done | Chaos: node kill, partition, heal verification | `make test-chaos` passes: cluster scenarios next to the single-node ones | `test/chaos/` |

Out of scope for milestone 3, recorded so nobody assumes otherwise: adding storage nodes
or drives to a formed cluster (new erasure sets), starting a node with one of its drives
offline, the Vault KMS adapter (4.4 — a cluster shares a sealed key file until then, and
says so at start), an operator surface for join/leave beyond the node RPC (5.x).

## Milestone 4 — IAM

Design (ADR-0006): users belong to groups; users and groups hold roles; a role is a
named set of policies; a policy is an IAM-style JSON document. An API key belongs to a
user and may carry an inline policy that can only narrow what its owner may do. The
identity of a request - key, owner, every policy that applies - is resolved in one
consistent read at authentication and travels in the principal; the one authorizer
decides on it. Everything is replicated through Raft; every IAM change carries its
audit record in the same entry.

| ID | Status | Step | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 4.1 | done | IAM domain: users, groups, roles, policies | IAM-style JSON; default deny; explicit deny wins; conditions; fuzz + property tests for policy parsing and evaluation | `internal/domain/iam/` |
| 4.2 | done | Policy-backed `ports.Authorizer` | one decision point; no authorization logic in handlers; a key never exceeds its owner; denials audited | `internal/ports/iam.go`, `internal/app/authz.go`, `internal/adapters/raftmeta/iam.go` |
| 4.3 | done | API keys | secrets envelope-encrypted through the KMS, shown once; expiry, inline policy, last-used, immediate revocation; S3 requests authenticated with them | `internal/app/iam.go`, `internal/app/identity.go`, `internal/adapters/s3api/` |
| 4.4 | done | Vault Transit KMS adapter | `--kms vault`; integration test against Vault via testcontainers | `internal/adapters/kms/vault/`, `cmd/cactusfs/` |
| 4.5 | done | Built-in roles | `cluster-admin`, `bucket-admin`, `read-write`, `read-only`, `auditor`; cannot be changed or removed | `internal/domain/iam/builtin.go` |
| 4.6 | done | Audit log | append-only, hash-chained in the replicated state; IAM mutations (atomically with the change), authentication failures, denials; audit events in the AsyncAPI contract; export as JSON lines | `internal/domain/audit/`, `internal/app/audit.go`, `internal/adapters/audit/`, `api/asyncapi/events.yaml` |
| 4.7 | done | Console users | Argon2id local accounts behind `ports.IdentityProvider`; replicated sessions, short-lived; `HttpOnly`/`Secure`/`SameSite=Strict` cookie and CSRF token helpers | `internal/app/sessions.go`, `internal/adapters/passwords/`, `internal/adapters/httpsession/` |

Out of scope for milestone 4: the HTTP surface for managing IAM (admin API, milestone
5 - until then IAM is reachable through the use cases only), OIDC, SSE-KMS/SSE-C,
bucket policies (6.x).

## Milestone 5 — Admin API, Swagger UI, console

Design: ADR-0007. The contract comes first: nothing below 5.1 is written before
`api/openapi/admin.yaml` says what it is.

| Step | Status | What | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 5.1 | done | OpenAPI contract and tooling | `api/openapi/admin.yaml` (OpenAPI 3.0.3, the highest `oapi-codegen` fully supports); `oapi-codegen` pinned as a `tool` directive; `oasdiff` pinned by digest in the builder image; both wired into `make gen` and `make contracts`; generated code committed | `api/openapi/admin.yaml`, `go.mod`, `Dockerfile`, `scripts/{gen,contracts}.sh` |
| 5.2 | done | Use cases the API needs | cluster status behind the authorizer (`admin:GetClusterStatus`); `Idempotency-Key` records replicated through Raft, responses stored sealed through the KMS; sign-in attempts limited per address | `internal/app/{clusterstatus,idempotency,sessions}.go`, `internal/ports/idempotency.go`, `internal/adapters/raftmeta/idempotency.go` |
| 5.3 | done | Admin API adapter | generated strict handlers under `/api/v1`; API key as bearer credential or console session with CSRF; every error is `application/problem+json`; `Idempotency-Key` on every mutation; spec served at `/api/v1/openapi.json` and `.yaml`; no authorization decision in the adapter | `internal/adapters/adminapi/` |
| 5.4 | done | Swagger UI at `/docs` | assets embedded with `go:embed`, nothing fetched from elsewhere (CSP enforces it); working Authorize button for API keys | `internal/adapters/adminapi/swaggerui/` |
| 5.5 | done | Embed the console | `-tags console` embeds `web/dist`; without the tag a stub page says the console was not built; served by nodes with the `console` role | `web/embed_*.go`, `internal/adapters/console/` |
| 5.6 | done | Generated web client | `openapi-typescript` + `openapi-fetch`, generated by `make gen`, committed; no hand-written API calls | `web/src/api/` |
| 5.7 | done | Console screens | sign-in, buckets, object browser, users, groups, roles and policies, API keys, cluster and drive status, audit log; responsive; brand assets used as they are; no business or authorization logic | `web/src/` |
| 5.8 | done | Wiring, tests, docs | admin API on the gateway role's admin listener; integration test over a real cluster; ADR-0007, ARCHITECTURE, README, threat model | `cmd/cactusfs/`, `test/integration/`, `docs/` |

## Milestone 6 — S3 depth

Built in this order; each step lands green on its own. Designs: ADR-0008 (versions in
the metadata state, entry schema v2), ADR-0009 (Object Lock and the clock that decides),
ADR-0010 (key hierarchy for multipart, SSE-KMS, SSE-C), ADR-0011 (resource policies,
anonymous access, CORS preflight), ADR-0012 (multipart uploads), ADR-0013 (lifecycle). `make test-chaos` before
every commit that touches the write path or the state machine.

| Step | Status | What | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 6.3 | done | Presigned URLs and signed streaming SigV4 | AWS documentation vectors verify; SDK presigner in the compat suite; fuzz tests | `internal/adapters/s3api/{sigv4,chunked,payload,handler}.go` |
| 6.0 | done | Foundations (entry schema 2 and the leader's time; strict XML decoder, with 6.2; extent refactor of shard storage, with 6.1; the routing table was not built - see Decisions) | entry schema v2 gate (`MetadataVersion` 2, state schema 2 written by the first v2 entry); leader-stamped time `entry.At` with a high-water mark in the state; `applyEntry` receives the Raft index; `object.Extent` refactor of shard storage; per-operation allow-lists of query parameters in the S3 router (kept as a switch, not the table first planned); strict bounded XML decoder | `internal/adapters/raftmeta/{entry,fsm,store}.go`, `internal/app/storage.go`, `internal/domain/object/`, `internal/adapters/s3api/` |
| 6.2 | done | Versioning | `objects/<bucket>/<key>` unchanged (current, non-marker version); new nested `versions/<bucket>` keyed `enc(key) ‖ ^seq ‖ vid` for noncurrent versions and delete markers; `ensureDataIndex` walks versions; bucket versioning state; `versionId` on GET/HEAD/DELETE; ListObjectVersions; DeleteObjects; heal and scrub walk versions; upgrade test from an M5 state (unit and with the previous binary); property test of the state machine against a model | `internal/domain/{object,bucket}/`, `internal/ports/metadata.go`, `internal/adapters/raftmeta/`, `internal/app/{objects,heal,scrub,buckets}.go`, `internal/adapters/s3api/` |
| 6.4 | done | Object Lock (governance, compliance, legal hold) | enabled only at bucket creation (turns versioning on); default retention; checks in the domain, called by the use case and, authoritatively, by the state machine on the leader-stamped time; a compliance-locked version cannot be deleted or shortened by anyone, root included; `s3:BypassGovernanceRetention` is never granted by a wildcard; property test over arbitrary command sequences | `internal/domain/object/lock.go`, `internal/domain/bucket/bucket.go`, `internal/adapters/raftmeta/`, `internal/app/`, `internal/adapters/s3api/` |
| 6.1 | done | Multipart upload | each part its own encrypted, erasure-coded extent; one KMS data key per upload, HKDF-derived key per part; `uploads` and `parts` buckets; Complete validates and commits atomically at apply; range reads across parts; `md5-of-md5s-N` ETag; unlisted and aborted parts collected; the part list of an object sealed (ADR-0010); UploadPartCopy deferred (501) | `internal/app/multipart.go`, `internal/domain/object/multipart.go`, `internal/adapters/raftmeta/uploads.go`, `internal/adapters/s3api/multipart.go`, `internal/ports/metadata.go` |
| 6.5 | done | SSE-KMS and SSE-C | alongside SSE-S3; SSE-C key combined with a KMS data key by HKDF and never stored (a check value is; no digest of the key, and no digest of the plaintext); refused without TLS, unsigned, or in a presigned query; SSE-KMS with the one configured key binds an encryption context | `internal/app/objects.go`, `internal/domain/object/`, `internal/adapters/s3api/` |
| 6.7 | done | Bucket policy, CORS, tagging | resource policy with Principal as a type of its own; anonymous access only when a bucket policy grants it, and only for read actions; explicit deny wins across identity and bucket policies; a key's inline policy still bounds it; root not bound; CORS preflight without authentication and without an existence oracle; object and bucket tags | `internal/domain/iam/`, `internal/domain/bucket/`, `internal/app/`, `internal/adapters/s3api/` |
| 6.6 | done | Lifecycle | expiration by days or date, noncurrent version expiration, abort of incomplete uploads, expired delete markers; worker on the metadata leader; deletes go through the normal use cases as a service principal and never bypass Object Lock; transitions and tag or size filters refused | `internal/domain/bucket/lifecycle.go`, `internal/domain/iam/iam.go`, `internal/app/{lifecycle,buckets,authz}.go`, `internal/adapters/s3api/{lifecycle,versioning}.go`, `internal/adapters/raftmeta/{entry,versions,store}.go`, `internal/ports/metadata.go`, `internal/adapters/telemetry/`, `cmd/cactusfs/{config,node}.go` |
| 6.9 | done | CopyObject, UploadPartCopy, ListObjects v1, conditional headers | a copy is a read of the source and a write of the destination, each authorized as what it is, re-encrypted under keys of its own; metadata and tagging directives; copy-source version, range, conditions and customer key; `If-Match`, `If-None-Match`, `If-Modified-Since`, `If-Unmodified-Since` on GET and HEAD, judged only once the caller is known to be able to read; ListObjects v1 by marker; conditional writes stay 501 | `internal/domain/object/conditions.go`, `internal/app/objects.go`, `internal/adapters/s3api/{copy,handler,operations,sse}.go` |
| 6.8 | done | aws-cli and ceph/s3-tests in the compat suite | server started with four drives; the AWS CLI and ceph/s3-tests in a stage of the builder image, each pinned by the checksum of what is downloaded and the Python packages by hash; one Go test per AWS CLI task in `baseline.txt`; an explicit list of s3-tests (`tools/s3tests-selected.txt`) with a baseline of its own (`tools/s3tests-baseline.txt`); built only in the builder (tag `s3tools`), so `go test -tags=s3compat` on a host still needs Go alone | `test/s3compat/{awscli,ceph,main}_test.go`, `test/s3compat/tools/`, `Dockerfile`, `scripts/{s3compat.sh,fetch.py}`, `internal/adapters/s3api/` |

## Goal A — default console user (Albie, 2026-10-11)

| ID | Status | Step | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|

## Goals B, C, D — one change of schema (Albie, 2026-10-11)

B: who uploaded an object, when, how often it was downloaded and by whom. C: search, sub-buckets.
D: tags in the console, rules for tags, a catalogue of tags. Planned with the `planner` subagent
(twice). All three change replicated state, so they share ONE rise of the entry schema, to 3, one
fixture written by the schema-2 binary, and one rolling-upgrade test. Order: S.1, S.2, the domain
steps (B.1, C.1, D.1), S.3, S.4, then B, C, D. `make test-chaos` before S.3, S.4, B.3, C.7, D.7 are
done.

objects, bucket size in GB, created date, who created it and the policies attached to the bucket,

## Goal H — settings: defined once, shown for every node (Albie, 2026-10-12)

"Define the .env file settings. Add a settings menu item to show all settings for all nodes
and cactusfs global settings e.g. each node volume mapped etc." Planned with the `planner`
subagent. No change of entry or state schema: a node's settings are read from the node when
asked (node RPC `Settings`), never stored. Starts after 7.5 and 7.7/7.8 are committed. After
H.1: H.2 and H.3 beside H.4-H.6; H.7 and H.8 beside each other; H.11 once H.5 is in.
`make test-chaos` is not required (no write path, quorum, Raft or healing).

## Goal I — a load balancer in front of CactusFS, documented (Albie, 2026-10-12)

"Document how to use a loadbalancer for docker desktop, cluster deployment on virtual
machines, and k3s or k8s." Documents and examples only; no code.

| ID | Status | Step | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| I.1 | done | The guide | what a balancer must and must not do, from the code: ports 9000 and 9001 only, never 9002/9003; Host, path and query kept (SigV4); no buffering, no cap below 5 GiB, long timeouts; TLS passed through or re-encrypted, never plain; `/readyz` over HTTPS on 9001; a check quicker than the drain delay; no stickiness (sessions are in the metadata); then compose, virtual machines (HAProxy and keepalived), k3s and Kubernetes (LoadBalancer, MetalLB, NodePort, Ingress or Gateway API with passthrough; the NetworkPolicy's `clientsFrom`/`adminFrom`); verifying; limits and gaps | `docs/load-balancing.md` |
| I.2 | done | Examples | nine files, each marked an example; the compose one run in front of the four-node cluster on the Docker host, `nginx -t` on the nginx one; the rest parsed only, and said so | `deploy/examples/loadbalancer/` |

## Milestone 7 — Deployment hardening

Planned with the `planner` subagent on 2026-10-11. Order of work: 7.0, 7.1, 7.6, 7.2,
7.7, 7.8, 7.4, 7.5, 7.3, 7.10, 7.9, 7.11. No new Go dependency is planned; the tools
below live in the builder image only, pinned by digest or checksum.

| ID | Status | Step | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 7.0 | done | Listeners open before the node has joined its cluster | a node whose peers cannot be reached answers `/healthz` 200 and `/readyz` 503 within 5 s; S3 answers 503 `ServiceUnavailable` and the admin API 503 problem+json until it has joined; nothing is served without authentication that was not before; `make test-chaos` green | `cmd/cactusfs/{server,node,cluster}.go` + tests, `test/integration/`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` |
| 7.1 | done | Helm chart with pools, and manifest validation offline | `make helm-lint`: `helm lint --strict`, renders of `ci/*.yaml` (4 nodes with 3 voters; 5 voters; standalone) validated by `kubeconform -strict` against schemas in the builder image, network off; `scripts/helm-assert.sh` asserts per render: headless Service with `publishNotReadyAddresses`, a claim per drive and one for metadata, PDB, topology spread, requests/limits, three probes, `runAsNonRoot`, `readOnlyRootFilesystem`, `drop: [ALL]`, `seccompProfile: RuntimeDefault`, no service-account token, no storage class, Ingress or cloud annotation by default; the render fails for a number of voters other than 3 or 5, for the local KMS on more than one node without an explicit development value, and when no Secret is named for root credentials or TLS | `deploy/helm/cactusfs/**`, `scripts/{helm-lint,helm-assert}.sh`, `Dockerfile`, `README.md`, ADR-0015 |
| 7.2 | done | Default-deny NetworkPolicy | a policy that selects every pod with both policy types and no rule, and allows: 9000 from the peers named for S3 and 9001 from the peers named for the admin port, each apart (none by default), 9002/9003 between the chart's pods only, egress to the chart's pods, DNS and the configured Vault and OTLP addresses only; asserted in `helm-assert.sh` | `deploy/helm/cactusfs/templates/networkpolicy.yaml`, `values.yaml`, `scripts/helm-assert.sh` |
| 7.3 | done | SBOM and signature, verified offline | `make sbom` writes SPDX JSON for the image of each architecture and for `web/pnpm-lock.yaml`; `make sign-verify` (in `make check`) pushes the image to a throw-away registry container, signs it with a key made for the run, verifies, attaches the SBOM as an attestation and verifies that, and proves that an unsigned and a re-tagged image fail verification | `scripts/{sbom,sign-verify}.sh`, `Makefile`, `Dockerfile`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, ADR-0021 |
| 7.4 | done | Observability pack | `make observability-check` (in `make check`): `promtool check rules` and `promtool test rules` pass; every metric a rule or dashboard names exists in the registry (a Go test reads both); dashboards are JSON with no remote asset; PodMonitor and PrometheusRule templates off by default | `deploy/observability/`, `deploy/helm/cactusfs/templates/`, `Dockerfile`, `Makefile`, `scripts/observability.sh` |
| 7.5 | done | Certificate rotation without restart | on the four-node cluster: node and S3 certificates replaced on disk; within the poll interval new handshakes present the new certificate, no request fails, Raft keeps its leader; a replacement that names another node or fails the CA is refused and logged, and the old one stays | `internal/adapters/nodetls/` + tests, `cmd/cactusfs/{server,cluster}.go`, `test/integration/`, ADR-0020 |
| 7.6 | done | Drain delay default in the chart | rendered `CACTUSFS_DRAIN_DELAY` covers readiness `periodSeconds x failureThreshold`; `terminationGracePeriodSeconds` exceeds drain plus shutdown timeout; asserted in `helm-assert.sh`; no `preStop` | `deploy/helm/cactusfs/`, `scripts/helm-assert.sh` |
| 7.7 | done | Node RPC: spans, metrics, trace propagation | with a span recorder in memory, a PUT through two nodes is one trace whose gateway and storage spans share a trace ID; `cactusfs_noderpc_requests_total{method,code}` and a duration on both sides; no key, bucket or secret in an attribute (test) | `internal/adapters/noderpc/`, `internal/adapters/telemetry/rpc.go` + tests, `cmd/cactusfs/cluster.go` |
| 7.8 | done | Remaining boundaries and state gauges | decorators for the IAM, session, idempotency, audit-log, event-publisher and password ports; gauges for Raft (leader, term, applied index), drives (online, bytes free), node-certificate expiry, refused handshakes; a test fails when a port is wired without its decorator; metric names listed in `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` and checked against the registry | `internal/adapters/telemetry/` + tests, `cmd/cactusfs/node.go`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` |
| 7.9 | done | Release workflow: signed manifest list, SBOM, binaries, chart | on a `v*` tag the workflow runs `scripts/release.sh` in the builder; `README.md` gives the command to verify. Albie, 2026-10-12: the module path stays, the registry is `ghcr.io/albievan/cactusfs`, signing is keyless, the licence declared is the blessing of `LICENSE.md`. What can be proved without them (`release.sh --dry-run` against the local registry) is part of 7.3 | `.github/workflows/release.yml`, `scripts/release.sh`, `README.md` |
| 7.10 | done | The chart on k3s | `make test-k8s` (build tag `k8s`, its own CI job, not in `make check`: k3s needs a privileged container): chart installed with the image built here; pods Ready; S3 round trip; a pod deleted, the object still read, the pod back; a pod outside the release cannot reach 9002/9003; pods admitted in a namespace that enforces the `restricted` standard | `test/k8s/`, `Makefile`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml` |

## Milestone 8 — Hybrid

Planned with the `planner` subagent on 2026-10-12. Three sub-milestones, each green and
reported on its own, on ONE rise of the entry and state schema, to 5 (ADR-0023), as schema 3
was for goals B-D: 8.0 (the fixture, the ADRs, every contract, the whole state machine, the
rolling upgrade, targets and the delivery worker), then 8A quotas (8.3.x), 8B notifications
(8.2.x), 8C replication (8.1.x), then 8.9. Designs: ADR-0023 (schema 5, usage counters,
outbox, targets, egress), ADR-0024 (quotas), ADR-0025 (notifications, audit export),
ADR-0026 (replication and the conflict rule).

Order: 8.0.1 first, from a clean `git worktree` of 84334a7, before any schema-5 code. Then
8.0.2, 8.0.3, 8.0.4, 8.0.5, 8.0.6, 8.0.7 to 8.0.9 in that order (one package), 8.0.10, 8.0.11.
Beside 8.0.6 to 8.0.11 (other files): 8.0.12. Then 8.0.13, 8.0.14. After 8.0: 8A and 8B side
by side except the steps that touch `cmd/cactusfs/node.go`, `internal/adapters/telemetry/` and
`cmd/cactusfs/settings.go` (8.3.3, 8.2.5, 8.1.7: one at a time). 8.1.1 and 8.1.2 may run
beside 8B; 8.1.4 onward after 8.2.5. `make test-chaos` before 8.0.7, 8.0.8, 8.0.9, 8.0.10,
8.3.2, 8.3.4, 8.1.1, 8.1.9 and 8.9 are done; `make test-rolling` at 8.0.11 and 8.9;
`make test-k8s` at 8.2.9 and 8.9. New container tests are in package `test/integration`,
never `t.Parallel`, one cluster a test file; the notification and replication tests add at
most two small containers (NATS, a receiver) or one standalone node to a four-node cluster.

### 8.0 — Groundwork: schema 5, the outbox, targets

Table test of `versionOf`, `heldTo`, `writtenAt` for every shape; a store held to 4 applies a put, a delete and a `bucket.create` to the same bytes as 84334a7; fuzz of the decoder | `internal/adapters/raftmeta/{entry,fsm,store}.go`, `internal/adapters/raftmeta/schema5_test.go` |

Tests: row for row against a model; determinism and snapshot as in 8.0.7; golden records; chaos green | `internal/adapters/raftmeta/{targets,outbox,bucketcfg,fsm}.go`, `schema5_test.go`, `export_test.go`, `testdata/` |
| 8.0.9 | done | State machine: replica mode, replica entries, status, resync record | `bucket.replica.put` (needs versioning `Enabled`, an exact usage record and no `replication` document; a `replication` document is refused on a replica bucket; versioning cannot be suspended under either); in a replica bucket a put, an upload creation, a completion and a delete that would make a marker are refused with `ErrInvalidBucketState` unless they carry `replica` from that source, and a delete of one version is judged as everywhere; a replica version or marker is stored at the source's `Seq` and is current exactly when no version of its key has a greater one; a second arrival of a version ID is `ErrConflict`; `replication` is `REPLICA` on arrival, `PENDING` when an `r:` consumer matched, and set by `outbox.ack` results only on a version that still exists; `resync`. Out of scope for milestone 8, recorded so nobody assumes otherwise: active-active replication
and automatic fail-back; more than one destination a bucket; replication of bucket
configuration, IAM and permanent deletes; SSE-C objects across sites; notification targets
defined by bucket owners; batched webhook posts; NATS credential files and client
certificates; a proxy for outbound calls; quotas by user, by tenant or by number of objects;
the client's address in a notification.

---

## Goal L — a session that locks, not ends, while objects are being uploaded (Albie, 2026-10-13)

"Do not time out the session while busy uploading objects; rather disable all functionality and
show a dialog box to type the user's password to enable all functionality - unless all objects
were uploaded and the session timed out: then log off from the system."

A session past its timeout whose user has an upload in flight is *locked*: the server lets it
store objects, read its own state, unlock and sign out, and answers everything else 423
`SessionLocked`. The lock is derived from the session record and an upload lease the gateways
keep in the replicated state with the commands that exist (ADR-0027): no entry schema, no record
and no file of `internal/adapters/raftmeta` changes. The S3 API is not touched; `make test-chaos`
is not owed (write path, quorum, Raft and healing are untouched).

| ID | Status | Step | Acceptance criterion | Files |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| L.0 | done | ADR-0027 and the threat-model rows, before code | `docs/adr/0027-session-lock.md` accepted by the reviewer against this section; indexed in `docs/adr/README.md`; ADR-0006's "sessions are fixed-length" and ADR-0007's session paragraph point to it; the four rows below are in `docs/threat-model.md` with status "planned (PLAN L)" | `docs/adr/0027-session-lock.md`, `docs/adr/README.md`, `docs/adr/0006-iam.md`, `docs/adr/0007-admin-api.md`, `docs/threat-model.md` |
| L.1 | done | Contract first: `unlockSession`, the session's state, the problem | `api/openapi/admin.yaml` has `POST /session/unlock` (`operationId: unlockSession`, `security: [{session: []}]`, body `UnlockRequest{password}` required, max 1024; 200 = `Session` + `Set-Cookie`; `IdempotencyKey` "accepted and has no effect", `CSRFToken`); `Identity` gains optional `locked` (boolean), `expires_at`, `lock_ends_at` (date-time; only with `via: session`); the description names 423 `SessionLocked` and, for unlock, 403 `AccessDenied`, 429 `TooManyAttempts`, 401; `make gen` leaves no diff and `make contracts` is green (`oasdiff breaking`: none); the operation answers 501 and is listed in `TestOperationsThatAreNotWrittenYet` until L.6 | `api/openapi/admin.yaml`, `api/openapi/admin.json`, `internal/adapters/adminapi/api/api.gen.go`, `internal/adapters/adminapi/operations.go`, `internal/adapters/adminapi/handler_test.go`, `web/src/api/schema.d.ts` |

Table tests in `authz_test.go` through the real `Authorizer`, including a request scope primed for a search | `internal/app/authz.go`, `internal/app/authz_test.go` |

Property: `uploadLeaseKey` never has the form of `hashToken`. App coverage >= 80 % | `internal/app/sessions.go`, `internal/app/sessions_test.go` (new), `internal/app/export_test.go`, `internal/app/iam.go`, `internal/app/iam_test.go` |

Table tests: right / wrong / throttled / fifth wrong / on an active session / of an ended session / password over `maxPasswordLength` | `internal/app/sessions.go`, `internal/app/sessions_test.go` |
| L.6 | done | Admin API adapter | `pipeline` calls `Sessions.UploadBegun` for `patternUpload` authenticated by a session and never runs `idempotent` for `patternUnlock`; `UnlockSession` sets the new cookie; a bearer caller gets 400; `GetSession` fills `locked`, `expires_at`, `lock_ends_at`; sign-in and unlock set the cookie's `Expires` to `CookieExpires`. Tests: `TestEveryRequestIsAuthenticated` covers unlock; new `TestALockedSessionOnlyUploads` walks the contract as that test does - a well-formed call of every operation that needs a credential, bar `getSession`, `signOut`, `unlockSession`, `putObject`, answers 423 `SessionLocked` (501 only for those of `TestOperationsThatAreNotWrittenYet`), never 2xx, and leaves an audit record; `putObject` answers 200 and, for a user without `s3:PutObject`, 403; unlock without `X-CSRF-Token` is refused; unlock sent twice with one `Idempotency-Key` is carried out twice and leaves no idempotency record; wrong password 403, throttled 429 with `Retry-After`; an API key is never locked | `internal/adapters/adminapi/handler.go`, `internal/adapters/adminapi/operations.go`, `internal/adapters/adminapi/handler_test.go`, `internal/adapters/httpsession/httpsession.go` (doc comment only) |
| L.7 | done | Settings and wiring | one row each in `serverSettings()`, group `sessions` ("Console sessions"), scope cluster: `session-lifetime` / `CACTUSFS_SESSION_LIFETIME` (30m; 10s to 12h), `session-upload-grace` / `CACTUSFS_SESSION_UPLOAD_GRACE` (90s; 5s to 10m), `session-lock-max` / `CACTUSFS_SESSION_LOCK_MAX` (12h; 0 = a session never locks; at most 24h); a value out of range fails the start with the setting named; `node.go` passes them and runs `n.sessions.Run` in `n.background`; `make settings-docs` leaves no diff; `GET /settings` shows the three; `localsettings_test.go` and `wiring_test.go` pass | `cmd/cactusfs/config.go`, `cmd/cactusfs/settings.go`, `cmd/cactusfs/node.go`, `cmd/cactusfs/*_test.go`, `deploy/cactusfs.env.example`, `README.md`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` |
| L.8 | done | Integration: the binary, a short timeout, a slow upload | `test/integration/session_lock_test.go` (tag `integration`), lifetime 10s, grace 5s, lock max 1m. (a) sign in, begin a `PUT` whose body arrives over 15 s; past the timeout `GET /buckets` = 423 `SessionLocked`, `GET /session` = 200 `locked: true`, a second `PUT` begun now = 200, the first `PUT` = 200; unlock with a wrong password = 403; with the right one = 200 and a new cookie; the old cookie = 401; `GET /buckets` = 200; the audit trail holds the denial, the failure and the unlock. (b) the same, nobody unlocks: within grace + 2 s of the upload's end `GET /session` = 401. (c) no upload: 401 at the timeout. (d) root changes the user's password with `PUT /users/{name}`: the locked session and an active one answer 401, unlock with the new password 401, the upload in flight is stored, the old password signs in no more, and the new one does once `PasswordChangeMargin` (5 s) has passed. (L.8b, with `make test-rolling`: a member of the previous release answers 401 for a locked session and its sweep breaks nothing. Not covered: a change of password in a mixed deployment, a leader of this release with gateways of the previous one. It runs with the Makefile's `-run` prefix of today; once 8.0.11 selects one test by exact name it must be named - `make test-rolling ROLLING_FROM_COMMIT=0b7d825 ROLLING_TEST=TestRollingUpgradeFromSchema3ToSchema4SessionLock` - as its comment says) | `test/integration/session_lock_test.go`, `test/integration/session_lock_rolling_test.go` |
| L.9 | done | Console: locked is not ended | `client.ts`: `onLocked(listener)`, told of a 423 by the middleware and by `uploadObjectWithProgress`; a 401 as before, but for one of an older generation of tokens (below). `App`: `signed-in` carries `locked` and `expiresAt`; a timer at `expires_at` and every 423 ask `GET /session` again; while locked it is asked every 15 s; `UploadsProvider` and `NoticesProvider` stay mounted, `Layout` and `Screens` are not rendered. `LockScreen`: `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, focus in the password field (`type="password"`, `autocomplete="current-password"`), the user's name as text, "Unlock", "Sign out (stops the uploads)" which asks first while `uploads.busy()`, why it is locked, the uploads' progress with no control of it operable (`ActivityBar readOnly`); 403 = "The password is wrong", 429 = "Too many attempts - try again in a few minutes", both keep the lock; success stores the new CSRF token and shows the route that was shown. An unlock is given up after `UNLOCK_TIMEOUT_MS` (20 s, its request aborted): what waited for it is sent and the lock says "The server could not be reached. Try again."; sign-out (`DELETE /session`) never waits for an unlock, and an unlock it abandoned that the API still carries out has its session ended with its own tokens; every request is stamped with the generation of its tokens, and a 401 to one sent before an unlock and answered after it is not taken for the end of the session (an upload of that kind is an error of status 0, which the engine retries with the same key and the new tokens). A session that ends shows the sign-in screen with why, as far as the console can tell - "your session timed out" (a 401 to its own question at the timeout or while locked), "the password was wrong too many times" (a 401 to an unlock after four wrong passwords), otherwise "You were signed out." - and "N objects were stored, M were not" (no counts when there were no uploads) | `web/src/api/client.ts`, `web/src/App.tsx`, `web/src/screens/LockScreen.tsx` (new), `web/src/screens/SignIn.tsx`, `web/src/uploads/UploadsProvider.tsx`, `web/src/ui/ActivityBar.tsx`, `web/src/styles/console.css`, `web/src/test/fakeApi.ts` |
